# Reach roster, pass 5 — the Wikidata sweep. Every notable channel, not a list of them.
#
# WHY THIS PASS EXISTS
# reach-4 read ONE Wikipedia list (List of YouTubers, 947 rows) and resolved it
# through Wikidata property P2397. It worked, and it moved R from 22.7 to 27.6
# lower walls. But the list was the bottleneck, not the method: P2397 is a
# property on ~67,000 Wikidata items, and reach-4 touched 947 of them.
#
# So this pass drops the list and queries the property directly. Same recognition
# signal — an enwiki article is the notability test — applied to everything that
# carries it, instead of to whichever page someone had already compiled.
#
# COST: ZERO WIKIDATA QUOTA, AND THE YOUTUBE SIDE STAYS CHEAP.
# WDQS is free; the ids batch 50 to a quota unit. 66,908 ids hydrated for 1,326
# units against 10,000/day — 0.02 units per candidate, the same rate reach-4 hit,
# now at 70x the volume. Search, for comparison, costs 11.5.
#
# THE SCREEN, in the order applied. Everything above the line is STRUCTURAL —
# a Wikidata claim, not a regex over prose. reach-4 learned that a text screen
# over an encyclopedia's notes "catches 'political' and misses 'anti-ideology'";
# claims do not have that failure mode.
#
#   in SPARQL, before anything was fetched:
#     - has P2397 (YouTube channel id) and an English Wikipedia article
#     - no death date (P570)
#     - no Indian citizenship or country (P27/P17 = Q668) — the India hedge
#     - no public office held (P39), no political party (P102)
#     - no criminal conviction (P1399)
#     - not a politician, journalist, news presenter, adult performer or
#       televangelist (P106)
#
#   against live YouTube stats, after hydrating:
#     - the engine's own DEFAULT_FLOOR: >=1K subs, >=1K views, >=5 videos     -15,939
#     - `- Topic` auto-channels and VEVO label mirrors                         -5,530
#     - the region exclude on self-declared country                              -126
#     - a title-level editorial screen (adult, gambling, party politics)         -175
#
#   in a second free Wikidata pass:
#     - tied to an English-speaking territory: P27/P17/P495 in
#       {US, UK, CA, AU, NZ, IE}                                             -26,036
#     - not a broadcaster, TV/radio station, newspaper or news agency (P31)      -313
#
#   66,908 swept -> 16,887 kept.
#
# THE ANGLOPHONE CUT IS THE BIG ONE AND IT IS DELIBERATE.
# It removes more than the floor does, and it removes real, enormous creators —
# JuegaGerman, Fernanfloo, Luccas Neto, Masha and the Bear. They are not worse
# channels. They fail the standard catalog/excluded.txt already states: "a card
# works when a player recognizes the name", and this project's launch audience is
# anglophone. That file was already excluding channels one at a time for exactly
# this ("audience concentrated in one non-anglophone market"); doing it here, from
# a structured claim, is the same judgement applied before the quota is spent
# rather than after. It is revisable, and a second-language printing is where it
# would be revised.
#
# WHY THE COUNTRY COMES FROM WIKIDATA, NOT FROM YOUTUBE.
# YouTube's country is self-declared and ABSENT for 11,398 of the 43,236 channels
# that reached this cut — a quarter of the set. It cannot carry a decision this
# large. Wikidata's citizenship claim is present, sourced and stable, so the
# territory test reads that, and YouTube's field keeps doing the one job it is
# good for: the India exclude, where a self-declaration is the relevant fact.
#
# THE KNOWN LIMIT, stated because it is real. reach-4 hand-read all 336 survivors
# and cut 53 the automatic screen had missed. 16,887 cannot be hand-read, so this
# roster ships WITHOUT that pass. The structural screens are strictly stronger
# than reach-4's regex, but "stronger" is not "equivalent to a person reading it",
# and the honest expectation is that some cards here would not survive review.
# catalog/excluded.txt is the instrument for that, and it works after the fact.
#
# FORMAT  one UC id per line, `# name [subs]` for review. Grouped by band.
# ADDED   2026-08-02.
